Paired/fresh human and tactical checks run only under the confidence gate. Promotion mechanics (loop.py ~945): copy net to best.npz, state best_round=n, since_promotion=0, anchor/people refs set from record; refs auto re-score when the best round changes (loop.py ~734-751). Window is by round number (loop.py 348), so it doesn't depend on best. Self-play opponents = best_round-3..best_round-1. No manual-promotion tool; round 56 was done by hand (state.json manual_promotions). Plan if E92 passes: at a safe point stop, back up nets/round_K.npz for the last kept K, copy e85 net there and to best.npz, set best_round=K, since_promotion=0, anchor/people refs from E92 (or delete to re-score), add a manual_promotions entry, restart.
